Reading change: the ayah foreshadows vulnerability within grandeur.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3a160adcc773435a8c26","text":"89:21’s repeated `دَكًّا دَكًّا` retroactively changed the pillars into pre-crushed verticals; the later leveling made the earlier height feel structurally doomed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003","root_001043/B006","root_001043/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ba49233dd0d8c3067ca","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:7:3\",\"root_001043\",[\"B003\",\"B006\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sütunlar, yalnız mimari öğeler değil, yoğunlaşmış iktidarı taşıyan kurumsal dayanaklar olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5e7d60024e6f4c6f944a","text":"89:9 and 89:11 activated the unexpected hydraulic possibility: valley-channel plus overflow gave `ع م د B013` a coherent damming role. 89:12 then corrected the neutral “support” image by showing that what sustains a system can also sustain multiplied imbalance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003","root_001043/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9215efd036c6aadbceb9","text":"1. **Pillars as height and structural pride.** Lexical evidence: `ع م د B003` pillar or column; `ع م د B005` height and elevated structure; `س م و B001` rising high from the opening name-root. Structural cue: the phrase narrows Aad into Iram characterized by supports; later `أَوْتَاد` continues the fixation/support field. Assigned role: pillars embody visible elevation and load-bearing power. Abductive move: I infer that architecture is doing ideological work: height makes power appear durable. Reading change: Iram reads as a vertical civilization-image, not just a place label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B002","root_001043/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e49c6131df8aa3c4b45d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:7:3\",\"root_001043\",[\"B002\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yüksekliği ayakta tutan dayanak, ilerideki düzleme karşısında aynı yapının çöküş noktasına ve ezici belasına dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B013","root_001637/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ed571b1117e6503cc717","text":"2. **Exploratory: hydraulic control latent in the supports.** **Evidence/cue:** `ع م د B013` is blocking a stream so water gathers; 89:9 places cut rock in a valley (`و د ي B005`), and 89:11 activates overflow beyond measure through `ط غ ي B002`. **Mechanism:** the branch assigns the *ʿimad* a damming role within a carved valley system. The packet supplies supports, channel, and overflow; the claim that they form one hydraulic apparatus is abductive. **Reading change:** “possessor of the supports” can evoke control of gathered flow, making later excess a reversal of controlled water into uncontrollable social force.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f539e68e1660bb5af75e","text":"1. **Pillars as institutional mainstays.** **Evidence/cue:** `ع م د B003` supplies concrete columns, while `ع م د B006/B007` supplies a relied-on mainstay and an affair's central supporting part. The next ayat connect unmatched formation, rock cutting, stakes, bounded lands, and multiplied corruption. **Mechanism:** pillars carry both physical load and the social order built around monumental capacity.
